# Strata C++ -> Rust Test Porting Backlog

Unified backlog merged from the `Port now` (`MISSING` and `PARTIAL`) lists of all seven parity tables (`tests-a.md` through `tests-g.md`).

- **Rows total**: `814` (`394 PARTIAL`, `420 MISSING`; `20` taken by `RT1`, `605` ready in Section 2, `189` deferred to Section 3 for seams/production wiring).
- **Danger**: `data` (survey data lost or corrupted), `coordinates` (wrong position or CRS), `output` (wrong file handed in: SHP, PDF, package), `behaviour` (function does wrong thing), `looks` (UI look/layout/chrome).
- **Size**: `S` (one assertion set, no fixture), `M` (needs a fixture file, temp survey, or mock local server), `L` (needs a seam or production code change first: named with `Seam-*` and listed in Section 3).
- **RT1**: Rows `#1`–`#20` of `tests-a.md` (`Port now`) carry `taken by RT1` in the last column and are excluded from Section 2 porting orders (`RT2..`).

## Section 2: Porting orders

Tests-only groups of size `S`/`M` rows (excluding `tests-a.md #1`–`#20` already taken by `RT1` and all `L` rows deferred to Section 3). Ordered from highest danger (`data` -> `coordinates` -> `output` -> `behaviour` -> `looks`). Every group owns a single crate or disjoint test-file family so all non-conflicting groups can run in parallel without production code edits.

## Could not determine

1. **`tests-d.md` `#9` and `#10` placement under risk block 1**: In `docs/parity/tests-d.md` (`Port now`), `#9` (`test_reference_download.cpp:519`, `geologyResponseCrsPreservesSurveyLocation`) and `#10` (`test_heritage_region.cpp:151`, `fiveKmScopeNeedsAMetricCrs`) sit above `#11` (`Data loss/corruption`) yet are explicitly tagged `*Wrong coordinates*`. Classified as `coordinates` here while preserving source citation `tests-d.md #9` / `#10`.
2. **`tests-d.md` `#27`, `#154`, `#155` and `tests-a.md` `#7` intentional Rust design differences**: `tests-d.md` marks `key_stores.rs` as intentionally read-only and injected (`#27` `scopeFolder_migratesLegacyNativeKey`, `#154` `scopeFolder_holdsEveryStore`, `#155` `scope_resetsToRealStores`), and `tests-a.md` marks `persistWorkspace_missingSurveyFile_writesLocalCopy` (`#7`, `지시 2E`) as intentionally refusing instead of writing a local copy. `#7` stays in Section 1 as `taken by RT1`; `#27`, `#154`, and `#155` stay in Section 3 (`Seam-D12`) unless reclassified `BLOCKED` by the director.
3. **Borderline `PARTIAL` rows needing small bug fixes or wiring (`tests-b.md #28, #35`, `tests-g.md #4`, `tests-e.md #10, #12`, `tests-a.md #41, #53`)**: Source table `note` columns state that `gui_tab_drawing_popups.rs:88` stores static `pos` instead of reprojecting on scale change (`tests-b #28`), `gui_tab_drawing_popups.rs:72` checks `sheet_rect` instead of `map_rect` with unwired Undo (`tests-b #35`), `export_sheet_pdf_atomic` (`sheet_pdf_export.rs:124`) has `simulate_disk_full` instead of an `AtomicBool` cancel flag and removes `out_pdf_path` (`tests-g #4`), `accounts.rs:135` `is_file()` lets a directory fall through to bundled INI (`tests-e #10`), and `topo_sheet.rs:135` ignores sheet ID/false-northing (`tests-e #12`). All of those are placed in Section 3 (`size: L`) so tests-only implementers are not blocked on production fixes; `tests-a #41` (`mask::redact` string masking without `SessionLog` file writer) stays in Section 2 while `#53` (global label font change UI not built) moves to Section 3 (`Seam-A1`).
4. **Single-crate or small file-family groups (`RT3`, `RT4`, `RT11`, `RT12`, `RT13`, `RT17`, `RT21`)**: Crates whose total remaining `S`/`M` backlog is under 15 rows (`strata-geo` 10, `strata-secrets` 10, `strata-engine` 6, `strata-canvas2d` 3, `strata-terrain3d` 12, `strata-paleo` 1) or the remaining file-family split of `strata-storage` (`RT13` 10) are kept strictly single-crate rather than mixed across crates.
5. **Source-table naive `check-tests.py` notices and parenthesized `BLOCKED (...)` labels**: Five source tables (`tests-a.md`, `tests-c.md`, `tests-d.md`, `tests-e.md`, `tests-g.md`) note that `docs/senior/tools/check-tests.py` undercounts Qt `private slots:` on inline helper structs, and `tests-g.md` adds parenthesized reasons to its `38 BLOCKED` per-file rows (`BLOCKED (assessment contract engine)`, etc.); all table totals in Section 4 reflect the verified per-file counts.
6. **`tests-f.md` `#36` (`keyExpressions_matchCppKeys`) and QGIS expressions**: `tests-f.md` notes Rust renders directly without QGIS expression strings (`feature_form.rs` has `kind_key`/`period_key`, which `#82` already covers). Placed in `RT28` (`strata-app`) as an S equivalence check on `kind_key`/`period_key`; if strict `QgsExpression` parsing is desired, move it to `Seam-F4` or mark `BLOCKED`.
7. **`tests-f.md` danger buckets (`[S1]`–`[S4]`)**: `tests-f.md` uses 4 severity levels (`S1` data -> `data`, `S2` coordinates -> `coordinates`, `S3` behaviour -> `behaviour`, `S4` looks -> `looks`) with no `output` tag; row `#18` (`layoutNumbersAreIndependentAndPersistInProject`) retains `behaviour` (`Seam-D9`) per `tests-f.md` so Section 4 counts agree 1:1 with `tests-f.md`.
8. **Intentional PaperLook / LegendGroup divergences in `tests-f.md` (`Seam-F1`–`Seam-F4`)**: Rows where `tests-f.md` documents missing persistence (`rust_settings.rs` ignoring `ui/highContrast`, `ui/largeText`, `ui/colorTheme`), startup `high_contrast: false` (`app_state_lifecycle.rs:28-31`), `shell_dialogs::render_close_prompt` hardcoding stock text while ignoring `ModalDialogController::format_close_prompt`, un-wired `s_draw_ref`/`FeaturePresets::load()`, un-wired per-layer label ink/halo in `gui_tab_map_survey.rs`, or single-column `LegendGroup` vs flat 2-column tree (`left_layer_panel.rs`) are placed in Section 3 (`Seam-F1`–`Seam-F4`) so `RT25`–`RT36` remain strictly tests-only.
